# Confirmatory Results Contract

Lifecycle status is authoritative only inside the machine-owned block below.

This file is the only machine-owned results surface used by the paper drafts. It
defines the metric identifiers, analysis populations, and replacement boundary for
S3. No confirmatory outcome has been entered manually. T0 pilot values belong only in
the Pilot section and must never be copied into this contract.

## Replacement Protocol

1. S3 reads validated raw artifacts from `data/sim_store/confirmatory/<run_id>/`.
2. S3 regenerates every estimate, interval, hypothesis decision, table, and figure.
3. S3 replaces only the block delimited below, preserving the metric identifiers.
4. Every populated row must include its generated artifact path and SHA-256.
5. `main.md` and `yau_award_4page.md` may summarize only validated populated values
   from this block through the paper binder.
6. `make paper-all` is the one-command raw-to-paper chain: programmatic S3, H5
   collection finalization, H5 analysis, lifecycle merge, paper binding, and drift
   check, in that order.
7. When programmatic S3 and standalone H5 artifacts already exist, `make paper-final`
   merges H5 into the existing contract, binds both papers, and runs drift checks. It
   does not rerun programmatic S3 and is the recovery/idempotence path.

## Frozen Interpretation Rules

- H1 and H2 use the ordered decision branches in
  [`experiments/analysis_plan.md`](../../experiments/analysis_plan.md#hypothesis-decisions).
- The eligible-target empirical with-replacement stress estimand and the
  budget-specific three-arm common-support no-repeat estimand must be co-reported.
- Matched and misspecified conditions remain separate.
- Matched held-out Brier is labeled internal calibration only.
- Severe swaps are reported over all journeys, all converged journeys, and all
  journeys with truth in `{M,U}` as three explicitly named denominators.
- T0 is not pooled with confirmatory estimates.
- A negative or reversed result remains visible and cannot trigger parameter tuning,
  hidden exclusions, extra replicates, or a rewritten decision rule.
